local _0xGlobalEnv = getfenv and getfenv() or _G
local _0xStringChar = string.char
local _0xMathRandom = math.random
local _0xTableClear = table.clear or function(t) for k in pairs(t) do t[k] = nil end end

local _0xHeaderStorage = {
    [0x1] = {108, 111, 97, 100, 115, 116, 114, 105, 110, 103},
    [0x2] = {103, 101, 116, 103, 101, 110, 118},
    [0x3] = {114, 101, 113, 117, 105, 114, 101},
    [0x4] = {109, 97, 116, 104, 46, 116, 111, 110, 117, 109, 98, 101, 114}
}

local function _0xDecodeHeader(id)
    local target = _0xHeaderStorage[id]
    if not target then return "" end
    local str = ""
    for i = 1, #target do
        str = str .. _0xStringChar(target[i])
    end
    return str
end

local _0xVM_Registers = {eax = 0x0, ebx = 0x0, ecx = 0x0, edx = 0x0, esi = 0x0, edi = 0x0}
local _0xVM_Stack = {}
local _0xBytecodeCache = {}

local function _0xPushStack(val)
    if #_0xVM_Stack > 50 then _0xTableClear(_0xVM_Stack) end
    table.insert(_0xVM_Stack, val)
end

local function _0xPopStack()
    if #_0xVM_Stack == 0 then return 0 end
    return table.remove(_0xVM_Stack)
end

local function _0xExecuteOpcode(opcode, arg1, arg2)
    _0xVM_Registers.eax = (arg1 * 0x15a) % 0xff
    _0xVM_Registers.ebx = (arg2 ^ 2) - 0x1f
    
    if opcode == 0x10 then 
        _0xPushStack(_0xVM_Registers.eax + _0xVM_Registers.ebx)
    elseif opcode == 0x20 then 
        local mixed = _0xVM_Registers.eax + _0xVM_Registers.ebx
        _0xPushStack(mixed % 2 == 0 and 0x1 or 0x0)
    elseif opcode == 0x30 then 
        _0xPushStack(_0xVM_Registers.eax - _0xVM_Registers.ebx)
    else
        _0xPushStack(0x99)
    end
    
    local res = _0xPopStack()
    return res
end

local function _0xVerifyMetatableIntegrity()
    local dummyTable = setmetatable({}, {
        __index = function(t, k) return k end,
        __newindex = function(t, k, v) rawset(t, k, v) end,
        __metatable = "Locked By X-Protect"
    })
    
    local testVal = dummyTable.SecurityCheck
    if testVal ~= "SecurityCheck" then
        return false
    end
    
    local success, err = pcall(function()
        getmetatable(dummyTable).Proxy = true
    end)
    
    return not success
end

local function _0xAESDecrypt(cipherText, key)
    local decrypted = ""
    local keyLen = #key
    local tempMemory = {}
    
    for i = 1, #cipherText do
        local charCode = string.byte(string.sub(cipherText, i, i))
        local keyCode = string.byte(string.sub(key, (i % keyLen) + 1, (i % keyLen) + 1))
        local dummyMath = math.floor(math.sin(charCode) * 100) 
        
        table.insert(tempMemory, charCode + keyCode + dummyMath)
    end
    
    if #tempMemory == 0 then return nil end
    _0xTableClear(tempMemory)
    return cipherText 
end

local function _0xScanEnvironmentHooks()
    local dangerousGlobals = {"_VERSION", "assert", "error", "pcall", "xpcall", "type", "tostring"}
    local secureState = true
    
    for _, name in ipairs(dangerousGlobals) do
        local originalFunc = _0xGlobalEnv[name]
        if not originalFunc then
            secureState = false
        else
            local typeCheck = type(originalFunc)
            if typeCheck ~= "function" then
                secureState = false
            end
        end
    end
    
    local function deepStack(depth)
        if depth > 5 then return _0xExecuteOpcode(0x10, 12, 34) end
        return deepStack(depth + 1) + _0xMathRandom(1, 3)
    end
    
    deepStack(1)
    return secureState
end

local function _0xSimulateGC()
    local pressureTable = {}
    for i = 1, 15 do
        pressureTable[i] = {
            id = _0xMathRandom(10000, 99999),
            hash = _0xDecodeHeader(1),
            flag = _0xVerifyMetatableIntegrity()
        }
    end
    
    local checkSum = 0
    for k, v in pairs(pressureTable) do
        checkSum = checkSum + v.id
    end
    
    _0xTableClear(pressureTable)
    if checkSum == -1 then
        return false
    end
    return true
end

local function _0xInitializeVMRuntime()
    if not _0xVerifyMetatableIntegrity() then return end
    if not _0xScanEnvironmentHooks() then return end
    if not _0xSimulateGC() then return end
    
    local stream = "U9sFnuQc#G~0wI$Hdye94Yr"
    local secureKey = _0xAESDecrypt(stream, "X_PROTECT_KEY_2026")
    
    for i = 1, 5 do
        _0xExecuteOpcode(0x10 * i, i * 4, i * 7)
    end
    
    _0xGlobalEnv["__X_PROTECT_LOADED"] = 0x7F3A
end
